spec_law_mult = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { is_country_type = decentralized }
		value = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_laissez_faire }
		value = 1.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_interventionism }
		value = 0.7
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_extraction_economy }
		value = 1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_agrarianism }
		value = 0.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_traditionalism }
		value = 0.5
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_industry_banned }
		value = 0.3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_command_economy }
		value = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership }
		value = 0
	}
}

spec_band_gain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf < 40 }
			value = 0.8
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf >= 80 }
			value = 1.3
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { var:spec_conf >= 60 }
			value = 1.15
		}
	}
}

spec_credit = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		add = var:spec_conf
	}
	subtract = 50
	divide = 50
	multiply = 1.5
	max = 0.15
	multiply = spec_pool_month
	multiply = spec_credit_rate
	max = spec_credit_ceiling
	max = spec_credit_room
	min = 0
}

spec_credit_preview = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		add = var:spec_conf
	}
	subtract = 50
	divide = 50
	multiply = 1.5
	max = 0.15
	multiply = spec_pool_month
	multiply = spec_law_mult
	multiply = spec_tech_credit
	multiply = spec_reform_credit
	multiply = spec_lax_credit
	multiply = spec_exch_credit
	multiply = spec_crunch_lend
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = spec_panic spec_crunch_lend <= 0 has_modifier = spec_ld_crash AND = { has_variable = spec_conf var:spec_conf < spec_panic_at } } }
		value = 0
	}
	max = spec_credit_ceiling
	max = spec_credit_room
	min = 0
}

spec_lev = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_debt
			spec_lev_income > 1
		}
		value = var:spec_debt
		divide = spec_lev_income
		min = 0
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_debt
			var:spec_debt > 0
			has_variable = spec_lev_last
		}
		value = var:spec_lev_last
	}
}

spec_lev_boom_mult = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_lev_held }
		value = var:spec_lev_held
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 0 var:spec_lev_held < 1 }
			subtract = 0
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 1 var:spec_lev_held < 2 }
			subtract = 1
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.08333
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 2 var:spec_lev_held < 3 }
			subtract = 2
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.16667
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 3 var:spec_lev_held < 4 }
			subtract = 3
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.25
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 4 var:spec_lev_held < 6 }
			subtract = 4
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.33333
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 6 var:spec_lev_held < 8 }
			subtract = 6
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.5
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 8 var:spec_lev_held < 10 }
			subtract = 8
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.66667
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 10 var:spec_lev_held < 12 }
			subtract = 10
			multiply = 0.08333
			add = 0.83333
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:spec_lev_held >= 12 }
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

	if = {

	if = {

spec_so_build_top = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { spec_bb_agriculture >= 1 spec_bb_extraction >= 1 spec_bb_heavy_industry >= 1 spec_bb_infrastructure >= 1 spec_bb_light_industry >= 1 spec_bb_military_industry >= 1 spec_bb_plantations >= 1 spec_bb_ranching >= 1 } }
		value = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { spec_bb_agriculture >= 2 spec_bb_extraction >= 2 spec_bb_heavy_industry >= 2 spec_bb_infrastructure >= 2 spec_bb_light_industry >= 2 spec_bb_military_industry >= 2 spec_bb_plantations >= 2 spec_bb_ranching >= 2 } }
		value = 0.2
	}
}

	}

spec_dev_aeroplanes = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:aeroplanes ?= {
			add = market_goods_pricier
		}
	}
}

spec_mv_aeroplanes = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:aeroplanes ?= {
			add = market_goods_sell_orders
		}
	}
	multiply = 80
}

spec_weight_aeroplanes = {
	value = spec_mv_aeroplanes
	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_aeroplanes = {
	value = var:spec_fast_aeroplanes
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_aeroplanes
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_aeroplanes = {
	value = var:spec_slow_aeroplanes
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_aeroplanes
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_aeroplanes = {
    value = var:spec_fast_aeroplanes
    subtract = var:spec_slow_aeroplanes
}

spec_supported_spread_aeroplanes = {
    value = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_defence }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_defence
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_aeroplanes >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_aeroplanes = {
    value = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_defence
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_aeroplanes >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_aeroplanes = 7
spec_review_has_aeroplanes = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_aeroplanes = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence } value = var:spec_review_defence } }
spec_review_reduction_aeroplanes = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_aeroplanes = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_aeroplanes divide = spec_price_spread_aeroplanes }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_aeroplanes = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_aeroplanes
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_aeroplanes >= var:spec_slow_aeroplanes }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_aeroplanes = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_aeroplanes >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_aeroplanes
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_aeroplanes
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_aeroplanes = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_aeroplanes spec_mv_aeroplanes > 0 var:spec_term_aeroplanes > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_aeroplanes spec_mv_aeroplanes > 0 var:spec_term_aeroplanes < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_aeroplanes = { value = spec_dev_aeroplanes multiply = spec_term_side_aeroplanes divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_aeroplanes = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_aeroplanes } value = var:spec_term_aeroplanes multiply = spec_term_side_aeroplanes divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_aeroplanes = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_aeroplanes add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_aeroplanes = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_aeroplanes divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_aeroplanes = { value = spec_term_supply_aeroplanes divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_aeroplanes = { value = spec_term_side_aeroplanes multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_aeroplanes multiply = spec_term_fade_aeroplanes multiply = spec_term_conf_aeroplanes multiply = spec_term_share_aeroplanes }

spec_term_tp_aeroplanes = { value = spec_term_effect_aeroplanes multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_aeroplanes = { value = spec_term_effect_aeroplanes add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_aeroplanes = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_aeroplanes
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_aeroplanes has_variable = spec_restrain_aeroplanes }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_aeroplanes
	}
}

spec_push_next_aeroplanes = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_aeroplanes }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_aeroplanes }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_aeroplanes
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_aeroplanes
}

spec_hype_next_aeroplanes = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_aeroplanes
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_aeroplanes
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_aeroplanes }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_aeroplanes
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_aeroplanes }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_aeroplanes = {
	value = var:spec_peak_aeroplanes
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_aeroplanes }
		value = var:spec_peak_aeroplanes
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_aeroplanes > var:spec_peak_aeroplanes }
		value = var:spec_hype_aeroplanes
	}
}

	subtract = spec_peak_next_aeroplanes
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_aeroplanes
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_aeroplanes
}

		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {

		value = 1
	}
}

		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_over_mult_aeroplanes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_aeroplanes >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_aeroplanes
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_over_mult_aeroplanes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_aeroplanes >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_aeroplanes
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_aeroplanes
		multiply = spec_over_mult_aeroplanes
	}
}

	max = 35

            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_automobiles >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_automobiles }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_automobiles }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_automobiles = {
    value = spec_price_spread_automobiles
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_transport }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_transport
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_automobiles >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_automobiles }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_automobiles }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_automobiles = 6
spec_review_has_automobiles = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_transport } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_automobiles = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_transport } value = var:spec_review_transport } }
spec_review_reduction_automobiles = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_automobiles = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_automobiles divide = spec_price_spread_automobiles }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_automobiles = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_automobiles
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_automobiles >= var:spec_slow_automobiles }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_automobiles = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_automobiles >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_automobiles
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_automobiles
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_automobiles = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_automobiles spec_mv_automobiles > 0 var:spec_term_automobiles > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_automobiles spec_mv_automobiles > 0 var:spec_term_automobiles < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_automobiles = { value = spec_dev_automobiles multiply = spec_term_side_automobiles divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_automobiles = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_automobiles } value = var:spec_term_automobiles multiply = spec_term_side_automobiles divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_automobiles = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_automobiles add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_automobiles = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_automobiles divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_automobiles = { value = spec_term_supply_automobiles divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_automobiles = { value = spec_term_side_automobiles multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_automobiles multiply = spec_term_fade_automobiles multiply = spec_term_conf_automobiles multiply = spec_term_share_automobiles }

spec_term_tp_automobiles = { value = spec_term_effect_automobiles multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_automobiles = { value = spec_term_effect_automobiles add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_automobiles = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_automobiles
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_automobiles has_variable = spec_restrain_automobiles }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_automobiles
	}
}

spec_push_next_automobiles = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_automobiles }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_automobiles }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_automobiles
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_automobiles
}

	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_automobiles

		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_automobiles }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_automobiles
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_automobiles }
		min = 1
	}
}

	add = 0.03

		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}

		limit = { var:spec_hype_automobiles > var:spec_peak_automobiles }
		value = var:spec_hype_automobiles
	}
}

	multiply = spec_weight_automobiles
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_automobiles
}

		multiply = spec_over_mult_automobiles

		value = var:spec_peak_automobiles

		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_automobiles
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_automobiles
		multiply = spec_over_mult_automobiles
	}
	else_if = {

		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_automobiles

            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_coal >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_coal }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_coal }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_coal = {
    value = spec_price_spread_coal
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_energy
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_coal >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_coal }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_coal }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_coal = 4
spec_review_has_coal = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_coal = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy } value = var:spec_review_energy } }
spec_review_reduction_coal = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_coal = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_coal divide = spec_price_spread_coal }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_coal = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_coal
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_coal >= var:spec_slow_coal }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_coal = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_coal >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_coal
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_coal
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_coal = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_coal spec_mv_coal > 0 var:spec_term_coal > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_coal spec_mv_coal > 0 var:spec_term_coal < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_coal = { value = spec_dev_coal multiply = spec_term_side_coal divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_coal = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_coal } value = var:spec_term_coal multiply = spec_term_side_coal divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_coal = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_coal add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_coal = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_coal divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_coal = { value = spec_term_supply_coal divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_coal = { value = spec_term_side_coal multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_coal multiply = spec_term_fade_coal multiply = spec_term_conf_coal multiply = spec_term_share_coal }

spec_term_tp_coal = { value = spec_term_effect_coal multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_coal = { value = spec_term_effect_coal add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_coal = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_coal
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_coal has_variable = spec_restrain_coal }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_coal
	}
}

spec_push_next_coal = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_coal }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_coal }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_coal
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_coal
}

spec_hype_next_coal = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_coal
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_coal
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_coal }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_coal
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_coal }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_coal = {
	value = var:spec_peak_coal
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_coal }
		value = var:spec_peak_coal
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_coal > var:spec_peak_coal }
		value = var:spec_hype_coal
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_coal = {
	value = var:spec_peak_coal
	subtract = spec_peak_next_coal
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_coal
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_coal
}

spec_sev_next_coal = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_coal = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_coal
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_coal
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_coal
		multiply = spec_over_mult_coal
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_coal
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_coal
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_coal
		multiply = spec_over_mult_coal
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_coal >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_coal
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_coal
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_coal
		multiply = spec_over_mult_coal
	}
}

spec_risk_next_coal = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_coal
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_dye }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_dye = 2
spec_review_has_dye = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_dye = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }
spec_review_reduction_dye = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_dye = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_dye divide = spec_price_spread_dye }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_dye = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_dye
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_dye >= var:spec_slow_dye }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_dye = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_dye >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_dye
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_dye
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_dye = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_dye spec_mv_dye > 0 var:spec_term_dye > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_dye spec_mv_dye > 0 var:spec_term_dye < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_dye = { value = spec_dev_dye multiply = spec_term_side_dye divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_dye = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_dye } value = var:spec_term_dye multiply = spec_term_side_dye divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_dye = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_dye add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_dye = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_dye divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_dye = { value = spec_term_supply_dye divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_dye = { value = spec_term_side_dye multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_dye multiply = spec_term_fade_dye multiply = spec_term_conf_dye multiply = spec_term_share_dye }

spec_term_tp_dye = { value = spec_term_effect_dye multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_dye = { value = spec_term_effect_dye add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_dye = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_dye
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_dye has_variable = spec_restrain_dye }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_dye
	}
}

spec_push_next_dye = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_dye }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_dye }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_dye
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_dye
}

spec_hype_next_dye = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_dye
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_dye
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_dye }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_dye
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_dye }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_dye = {
	value = var:spec_peak_dye
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_dye }
		value = var:spec_peak_dye
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_dye > var:spec_peak_dye }
		value = var:spec_hype_dye
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_dye = {
	value = var:spec_peak_dye
	subtract = spec_peak_next_dye
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_dye
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_dye
}

	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_dye = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_dye
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_dye
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_dye
		multiply = spec_over_mult_dye
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_dye
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_dye
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_dye
		multiply = spec_over_mult_dye
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_dye >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_dye
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_dye
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_dye
		multiply = spec_over_mult_dye
	}
}

	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_explosives = 5
spec_review_has_explosives = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_industry } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_explosives = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_industry } value = var:spec_review_industry } }
spec_review_reduction_explosives = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_explosives = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_explosives divide = spec_price_spread_explosives }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_explosives = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_explosives
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_explosives >= var:spec_slow_explosives }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_explosives = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_explosives >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_explosives
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_explosives
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_explosives = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_explosives spec_mv_explosives > 0 var:spec_term_explosives > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_explosives spec_mv_explosives > 0 var:spec_term_explosives < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_explosives = { value = spec_dev_explosives multiply = spec_term_side_explosives divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_explosives = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_explosives } value = var:spec_term_explosives multiply = spec_term_side_explosives divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_explosives = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_explosives add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_explosives = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_explosives divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_explosives = { value = spec_term_supply_explosives divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_explosives = { value = spec_term_side_explosives multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_explosives multiply = spec_term_fade_explosives multiply = spec_term_conf_explosives multiply = spec_term_share_explosives }

spec_term_tp_explosives = { value = spec_term_effect_explosives multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_explosives = { value = spec_term_effect_explosives add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_explosives = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_explosives
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_explosives has_variable = spec_restrain_explosives }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_explosives
	}
}

spec_push_next_explosives = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_explosives }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_explosives }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_explosives
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_explosives
}

	value = spec_hype_raw_explosives

		value = 0.4

	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_explosives }

	}

	add = 0.03

		add = 0.1

	multiply = spec_exp_mult_explosives

		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_explosives
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_explosives
		multiply = spec_over_mult_explosives
	}
	else_if = {

		value = var:spec_peak_explosives
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_explosives
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_explosives
		multiply = spec_over_mult_explosives
	}
}

        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_household
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_fine_art >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_fine_art }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_fine_art }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}

spec_review_mean_fine_art = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }

spec_term_gap_fine_art = { value = spec_dev_fine_art multiply = spec_term_side_fine_art divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_fine_art = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_fine_art } value = var:spec_term_fine_art multiply = spec_term_side_fine_art divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_fine_art = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_fine_art add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_fine_art = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_fine_art divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_fine_art = { value = spec_term_supply_fine_art divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_fine_art = { value = spec_term_side_fine_art multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_fine_art multiply = spec_term_fade_fine_art multiply = spec_term_conf_fine_art multiply = spec_term_share_fine_art }

spec_review_has_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }
spec_review_reduction_furniture = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_furniture = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_furniture divide = spec_price_spread_furniture }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_furniture = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_furniture
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_furniture >= var:spec_slow_furniture }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_furniture = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_furniture >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_furniture
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_furniture
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_furniture spec_mv_furniture > 0 var:spec_term_furniture > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_furniture spec_mv_furniture > 0 var:spec_term_furniture < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_furniture = { value = spec_dev_furniture multiply = spec_term_side_furniture divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_furniture } value = var:spec_term_furniture multiply = spec_term_side_furniture divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_furniture = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_furniture add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_furniture divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_furniture = { value = spec_term_supply_furniture divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_furniture = { value = spec_term_side_furniture multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_furniture multiply = spec_term_fade_furniture multiply = spec_term_conf_furniture multiply = spec_term_share_furniture }

spec_term_tp_furniture = { value = spec_term_effect_furniture multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_furniture = { value = spec_term_effect_furniture add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_furniture = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_furniture
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_furniture has_variable = spec_restrain_furniture }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_furniture
	}
}

spec_push_next_furniture = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_furniture }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_furniture }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_furniture
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_furniture
}

spec_hype_next_furniture = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_furniture
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_furniture
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_furniture }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_furniture
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_furniture }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_furniture = {
	value = var:spec_peak_furniture
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_furniture }
		value = var:spec_peak_furniture
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_furniture > var:spec_peak_furniture }
		value = var:spec_hype_furniture
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_furniture = {
	value = var:spec_peak_furniture
	subtract = spec_peak_next_furniture
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_furniture
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_furniture
}

		value = 3
	}

		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_furniture >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_furniture = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_furniture >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_furniture
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_furniture
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_furniture >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_furniture
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_furniture
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_furniture >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_furniture
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_furniture
	}
}

spec_review_mean_groceries = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_food } value = var:spec_review_food } }
spec_review_reduction_groceries = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_groceries = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_groceries divide = spec_price_spread_groceries }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_groceries = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_groceries
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_groceries >= var:spec_slow_groceries }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_groceries = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_groceries >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_groceries
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_groceries
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_groceries = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_groceries spec_mv_groceries > 0 var:spec_term_groceries > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_groceries spec_mv_groceries > 0 var:spec_term_groceries < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_groceries = { value = spec_dev_groceries multiply = spec_term_side_groceries divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_groceries = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_groceries } value = var:spec_term_groceries multiply = spec_term_side_groceries divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_groceries = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_groceries add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_groceries = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_groceries divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_groceries = { value = spec_term_supply_groceries divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_groceries = { value = spec_term_side_groceries multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_groceries multiply = spec_term_fade_groceries multiply = spec_term_conf_groceries multiply = spec_term_share_groceries }

spec_term_tp_groceries = { value = spec_term_effect_groceries multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_groceries = { value = spec_term_effect_groceries add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_groceries = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_groceries
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_groceries has_variable = spec_restrain_groceries }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_groceries
	}
}

spec_push_next_groceries = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_groceries }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_groceries }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_groceries
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_groceries
}

spec_hype_next_groceries = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_groceries
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_groceries
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_groceries }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_groceries
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_groceries }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_groceries = {
	value = var:spec_peak_groceries
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_groceries }
		value = var:spec_peak_groceries
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_groceries > var:spec_peak_groceries }
		value = var:spec_hype_groceries
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_groceries = {
	value = var:spec_peak_groceries
	subtract = spec_peak_next_groceries
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_groceries
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_groceries
}

spec_sev_next_groceries = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_groceries = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_groceries
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_groceries
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_groceries
		multiply = spec_over_mult_groceries
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_groceries
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_groceries
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_groceries
		multiply = spec_over_mult_groceries
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_groceries >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_groceries
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_groceries
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_groceries
		multiply = spec_over_mult_groceries
	}
}

spec_risk_next_groceries = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_groceries
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

spec_dev_hardwood = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:hardwood ?= {
			add = market_goods_pricier
		}
	}
}

spec_mv_hardwood = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:hardwood ?= {
			add = market_goods_sell_orders
		}
	}
	multiply = 40
}

spec_weight_hardwood = {
	value = spec_mv_hardwood
	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_hardwood = {
	value = var:spec_fast_hardwood
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_hardwood
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_hardwood = {
	value = var:spec_slow_hardwood
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_hardwood
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_hardwood = {
    value = var:spec_fast_hardwood
    subtract = var:spec_slow_hardwood
}

spec_supported_spread_hardwood = {
    value = spec_price_spread_hardwood
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_hardwood >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_hardwood }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_hardwood }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_hardwood = {
    value = spec_price_spread_hardwood
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_hardwood >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_hardwood }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_hardwood }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}

        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_lead = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_lead
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_lead >= var:spec_slow_lead }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_lead = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_lead >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_lead
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_lead
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_lead = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_lead spec_mv_lead > 0 var:spec_term_lead > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_lead spec_mv_lead > 0 var:spec_term_lead < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_lead = { value = spec_dev_lead multiply = spec_term_side_lead divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_lead = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_lead } value = var:spec_term_lead multiply = spec_term_side_lead divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_lead = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_lead add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_lead = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_lead divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_lead = { value = spec_term_supply_lead divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_lead = { value = spec_term_side_lead multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_lead multiply = spec_term_fade_lead multiply = spec_term_conf_lead multiply = spec_term_share_lead }

spec_term_tp_lead = { value = spec_term_effect_lead multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_lead = { value = spec_term_effect_lead add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_lead = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_lead
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_lead has_variable = spec_restrain_lead }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_lead
	}
}

spec_push_next_lead = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_lead }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_lead }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_lead
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_lead
}

spec_hype_next_lead = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_lead
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_lead
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_lead }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_lead
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_lead }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_lead = {
	value = var:spec_peak_lead
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_lead }
		value = var:spec_peak_lead
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_lead > var:spec_peak_lead }
		value = var:spec_hype_lead
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_lead = {
	value = var:spec_peak_lead
	subtract = spec_peak_next_lead
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_lead
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_lead
}

spec_sev_next_lead = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_lead = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_lead
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_lead
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_lead
		multiply = spec_over_mult_lead
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_lead
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_lead
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_lead
		multiply = spec_over_mult_lead
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_lead >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_lead
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_lead
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_lead
		multiply = spec_over_mult_lead
	}
}

spec_risk_next_lead = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_lead
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

	}

		}
	}
	multiply = 30
}

	value = spec_mv_liquor
	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

    value = var:spec_fast_liquor
    subtract = var:spec_slow_liquor
}

spec_supported_spread_liquor = {
    value = spec_price_spread_liquor
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_plantation }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_plantation
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_liquor >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_liquor }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_liquor }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_liquor = {
    value = spec_price_spread_liquor
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_plantation
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_liquor >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_liquor }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_liquor }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_liquor = 1
spec_review_has_liquor = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_liquor = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation } value = var:spec_review_plantation } }
spec_review_reduction_liquor = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_liquor = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_liquor divide = spec_price_spread_liquor }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_household
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_luxury_furniture >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_luxury_furniture }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_luxury_furniture }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_luxury_furniture = 2
spec_review_has_luxury_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_luxury_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }
spec_review_reduction_luxury_furniture = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_luxury_furniture = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_luxury_furniture divide = spec_price_spread_luxury_furniture }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_luxury_furniture = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_luxury_furniture
	multiply = 60

		limit = { var:spec_fast_luxury_furniture >= var:spec_slow_luxury_furniture }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}

		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}

spec_hype_raw_luxury_furniture = {
	value = 1

		limit = { spec_trend_luxury_furniture >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_luxury_furniture
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_luxury_furniture
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_luxury_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_luxury_furniture spec_mv_luxury_furniture > 0 var:spec_term_luxury_furniture > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_luxury_furniture spec_mv_luxury_furniture > 0 var:spec_term_luxury_furniture < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_luxury_furniture = { value = spec_dev_luxury_furniture multiply = spec_term_side_luxury_furniture divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_luxury_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_luxury_furniture } value = var:spec_term_luxury_furniture multiply = spec_term_side_luxury_furniture divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_luxury_furniture = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_luxury_furniture add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_luxury_furniture = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_luxury_furniture divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_luxury_furniture = { value = spec_term_supply_luxury_furniture divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_luxury_furniture = { value = spec_term_side_luxury_furniture multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_luxury_furniture multiply = spec_term_fade_luxury_furniture multiply = spec_term_conf_luxury_furniture multiply = spec_term_share_luxury_furniture }

spec_term_tp_luxury_furniture = { value = spec_term_effect_luxury_furniture multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_luxury_furniture = { value = spec_term_effect_luxury_furniture add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_luxury_furniture = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_luxury_furniture
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_luxury_furniture has_variable = spec_restrain_luxury_furniture }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_luxury_furniture
	}
}

	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_luxury_furniture }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_luxury_furniture }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_luxury_furniture
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_luxury_furniture
}

	multiply = spec_exp_mult_luxury_furniture

		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_luxury_furniture
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_luxury_furniture >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_luxury_furniture
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_luxury_furniture

		value = var:spec_peak_luxury_furniture
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_luxury_furniture
		multiply = spec_over_mult_luxury_furniture
	}

	max = 35

		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_merchant_marine
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_merchant_marine
		multiply = spec_over_mult_merchant_marine
	}
}

spec_risk_next_merchant_marine = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_merchant_marine
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

spec_dev_oil = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:oil ?= {
			add = market_goods_pricier
		}
	}
}

spec_mv_oil = {
	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:oil ?= {
			add = market_goods_sell_orders
		}
	}
	multiply = 40
}

spec_weight_oil = {
	value = spec_mv_oil
	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_oil = {
	value = var:spec_fast_oil
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_oil
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_oil = {
	value = var:spec_slow_oil
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_oil
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_oil = {
    value = var:spec_fast_oil
    subtract = var:spec_slow_oil
}

spec_supported_spread_oil = {
    value = spec_price_spread_oil
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_energy }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_energy
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_oil >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_oil }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_oil }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_oil = {
    value = spec_price_spread_oil
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_energy
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_oil >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_oil }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_oil }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_oil = 4
spec_review_has_oil = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_oil = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_energy } value = var:spec_review_energy } }
spec_review_reduction_oil = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_oil = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_oil divide = spec_price_spread_oil }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_oil = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_oil
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_oil >= var:spec_slow_oil }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_oil = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_oil >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_oil
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_oil
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_oil = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_oil spec_mv_oil > 0 var:spec_term_oil > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_oil spec_mv_oil > 0 var:spec_term_oil < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_oil = { value = spec_dev_oil multiply = spec_term_side_oil divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_oil = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_oil } value = var:spec_term_oil multiply = spec_term_side_oil divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_oil = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_oil add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_oil = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_oil divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_oil = { value = spec_term_supply_oil divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_oil = { value = spec_term_side_oil multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_oil multiply = spec_term_fade_oil multiply = spec_term_conf_oil multiply = spec_term_share_oil }

spec_term_tp_oil = { value = spec_term_effect_oil multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_oil = { value = spec_term_effect_oil add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_oil = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_oil
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_oil has_variable = spec_restrain_oil }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_oil
	}
}

spec_push_next_oil = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_oil }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_oil }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_oil
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_oil
}

spec_hype_next_oil = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_oil
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_oil
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_oil }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_oil
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_oil }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_oil = {
	value = var:spec_peak_oil
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_oil }
		value = var:spec_peak_oil
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_oil > var:spec_peak_oil }
		value = var:spec_hype_oil
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_oil = {
	value = var:spec_peak_oil
	subtract = spec_peak_next_oil
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_oil
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_oil
}

		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_oil >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

		multiply = spec_over_mult_oil

		multiply = spec_weight_oil
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_oil
		multiply = spec_over_mult_oil
	}
	else_if = {

	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_porcelain
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_slow_porcelain
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_porcelain
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_porcelain = {
    value = var:spec_fast_porcelain
    subtract = var:spec_slow_porcelain
}

spec_supported_spread_porcelain = {
    value = spec_price_spread_porcelain
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_household
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_porcelain >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_porcelain }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_porcelain }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_porcelain = {
    value = spec_price_spread_porcelain
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_household
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_porcelain >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_porcelain }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_porcelain }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_porcelain = 2
spec_review_has_porcelain = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_porcelain = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }
spec_review_reduction_porcelain = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_porcelain = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_porcelain divide = spec_price_spread_porcelain }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_porcelain = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_porcelain
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_porcelain >= var:spec_slow_porcelain }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_porcelain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_porcelain >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_porcelain
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_porcelain
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_porcelain = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_porcelain spec_mv_porcelain > 0 var:spec_term_porcelain > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_porcelain spec_mv_porcelain > 0 var:spec_term_porcelain < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_porcelain = { value = spec_dev_porcelain multiply = spec_term_side_porcelain divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_porcelain = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_porcelain } value = var:spec_term_porcelain multiply = spec_term_side_porcelain divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_porcelain = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_porcelain add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_porcelain = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_porcelain divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_porcelain = { value = spec_term_supply_porcelain divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_porcelain = { value = spec_term_side_porcelain multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_porcelain multiply = spec_term_fade_porcelain multiply = spec_term_conf_porcelain multiply = spec_term_share_porcelain }

spec_term_tp_porcelain = { value = spec_term_effect_porcelain multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_porcelain = { value = spec_term_effect_porcelain add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_porcelain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_porcelain
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_porcelain has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_porcelain
	}
}

spec_push_next_porcelain = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_porcelain }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_porcelain
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_porcelain
}

spec_hype_next_porcelain = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_porcelain
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_porcelain
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_porcelain }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_porcelain
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_porcelain }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_porcelain > var:spec_peak_porcelain }
		value = var:spec_hype_porcelain
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_porcelain = {
	value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
	subtract = spec_peak_next_porcelain
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
}

spec_sev_next_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_porcelain = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_porcelain >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_porcelain
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_porcelain
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_porcelain
		multiply = spec_over_mult_porcelain
	}
}

spec_risk_next_porcelain = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_porcelain
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

	market ?= {
		mg:radios ?= {
			add = market_goods_pricier
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

	max = 1

	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

	}

    subtract = var:spec_slow_silk
}

spec_supported_spread_silk = {
    value = spec_price_spread_silk
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_household
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_silk >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_silk }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_silk }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_silk = {
    value = spec_price_spread_silk
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {

            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_silk >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_silk }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_silk }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_silk = 2
spec_review_has_silk = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_silk = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_household } value = var:spec_review_household } }
spec_review_reduction_silk = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_silk = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_silk divide = spec_price_spread_silk }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_silk = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_silk
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_silk >= var:spec_slow_silk }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_silk = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_silk >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_silk
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_silk
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_silk = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_silk spec_mv_silk > 0 var:spec_term_silk > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_silk spec_mv_silk > 0 var:spec_term_silk < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_silk = { value = spec_dev_silk multiply = spec_term_side_silk divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_silk = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_silk } value = var:spec_term_silk multiply = spec_term_side_silk divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_silk = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_silk add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_silk = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_silk divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_silk = { value = spec_term_supply_silk divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_silk = { value = spec_term_side_silk multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_silk multiply = spec_term_fade_silk multiply = spec_term_conf_silk multiply = spec_term_share_silk }

spec_term_tp_silk = { value = spec_term_effect_silk multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_silk = { value = spec_term_effect_silk add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_silk = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_silk
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_silk has_variable = spec_restrain_silk }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_silk
	}
}

spec_push_next_silk = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_silk }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_silk }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_silk
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_silk
}

spec_hype_next_silk = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_silk
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_silk

		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_silk
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_silk }
		min = 1
	}

		add = 0.1
	}

		value = var:spec_hype_silk
	}

	value = var:spec_peak_silk

	multiply = spec_exp_mult_silk

	}

	}

		multiply = spec_exp_mult_silk
		multiply = spec_over_mult_silk
	}

		value = var:spec_peak_silk
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_silk
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_silk
		multiply = spec_over_mult_silk
	}
	else_if = {

		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_silk

	value = 0
	market ?= {
		mg:steel ?= {
			add = market_goods_sell_orders
		}
	}
	multiply = 50

	value = spec_mv_steel
	divide = var:spec_mv_total

	max = 1

spec_supported_spread_steel = {
    value = spec_price_spread_steel
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_steel >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_steel }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_steel }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_steel = {
    value = spec_price_spread_steel
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_steel >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_steel }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_steel }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_steel = 3
spec_review_has_steel = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_steel = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials } value = var:spec_review_materials } }
spec_review_reduction_steel = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_steel = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_steel divide = spec_price_spread_steel }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_steel = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_steel
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_steel >= var:spec_slow_steel }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_steel = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_steel >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_steel
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_steel
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_steel = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_steel spec_mv_steel > 0 var:spec_term_steel > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_steel spec_mv_steel > 0 var:spec_term_steel < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_steel = { value = spec_dev_steel multiply = spec_term_side_steel divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_steel = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_steel } value = var:spec_term_steel multiply = spec_term_side_steel divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_steel = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_steel add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_steel = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_steel divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_steel = { value = spec_term_supply_steel divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_steel = { value = spec_term_side_steel multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_steel multiply = spec_term_fade_steel multiply = spec_term_conf_steel multiply = spec_term_share_steel }

spec_term_tp_steel = { value = spec_term_effect_steel multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_steel = { value = spec_term_effect_steel add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_steel = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_steel
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_steel has_variable = spec_restrain_steel }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_steel
	}
}

spec_push_next_steel = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_steel }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_steel }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_steel
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_steel
}

spec_hype_next_steel = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_steel
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_steel
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_steel }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_steel
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_steel }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_steel = {
	value = var:spec_peak_steel
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_steel }
		value = var:spec_peak_steel
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_steel > var:spec_peak_steel }
		value = var:spec_hype_steel
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_steel = {
	value = var:spec_peak_steel
	subtract = spec_peak_next_steel
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_steel
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_steel
}

		value = 3

	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_steel >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_steel >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_steel = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_steel >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_steel
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_steel
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_steel
		multiply = spec_over_mult_steel
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_steel >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_steel
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_steel
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_steel
		multiply = spec_over_mult_steel
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_steel >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_steel
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_steel
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_steel
		multiply = spec_over_mult_steel
	}
}

	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_tanks = {
	value = var:spec_fast_tanks
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_tanks
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_tanks = {
	value = var:spec_slow_tanks
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_tanks
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_tanks = {
    value = var:spec_fast_tanks
    subtract = var:spec_slow_tanks
}

spec_supported_spread_tanks = {
    value = spec_price_spread_tanks
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_defence }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_defence
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_tanks >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_tanks }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_tanks }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_tanks = {
    value = spec_price_spread_tanks
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_defence
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_tanks >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_tanks }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_tanks }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_tanks = 7
spec_review_has_tanks = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_tanks = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_defence } value = var:spec_review_defence } }
spec_review_reduction_tanks = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_tanks = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_tanks divide = spec_price_spread_tanks }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_tanks = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_tanks
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_tanks >= var:spec_slow_tanks }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_tanks = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_tanks >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_tanks
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_tanks
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_tanks = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tanks spec_mv_tanks > 0 var:spec_term_tanks > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tanks spec_mv_tanks > 0 var:spec_term_tanks < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_tanks = { value = spec_dev_tanks multiply = spec_term_side_tanks divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_tanks = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tanks } value = var:spec_term_tanks multiply = spec_term_side_tanks divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_tanks = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_tanks add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_tanks = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_tanks divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_tanks = { value = spec_term_supply_tanks divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_tanks = { value = spec_term_side_tanks multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_tanks multiply = spec_term_fade_tanks multiply = spec_term_conf_tanks multiply = spec_term_share_tanks }

spec_term_tp_tanks = { value = spec_term_effect_tanks multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_tanks = { value = spec_term_effect_tanks add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_tanks = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_tanks
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_tanks has_variable = spec_restrain_tanks }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_tanks
	}
}

spec_push_next_tanks = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_tanks }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_tanks }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_tanks
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_tanks
}

spec_hype_next_tanks = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_tanks
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_tanks
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_tanks }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_tanks
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_tanks }
		min = 1
	}
}

	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03

		add = 0.1
	}

		limit = { var:spec_hype_tanks > var:spec_peak_tanks }
		value = var:spec_hype_tanks
	}
}

	multiply = spec_weight_tanks
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_tanks
}

		multiply = spec_over_mult_tanks
	}

		value = var:spec_peak_tanks
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_tanks
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_tanks
		multiply = spec_over_mult_tanks
	}
	else_if = {
