	divide = var:spec_mv_total
	divide = 0.03
	max = 1
}

spec_fast_next_tobacco = {
	value = var:spec_fast_tobacco
	multiply = 0.85
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_tobacco
		multiply = 0.15
	}
}

spec_slow_next_tobacco = {
	value = var:spec_slow_tobacco
	multiply = 0.95
	add = {
		value = spec_dev_tobacco
		multiply = 0.05
	}
}

spec_price_spread_tobacco = {
    value = var:spec_fast_tobacco
    subtract = var:spec_slow_tobacco
}

spec_supported_spread_tobacco = {
    value = spec_price_spread_tobacco
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_plantation }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_plantation
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_tobacco >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_tobacco }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_tobacco }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_tobacco = {
    value = spec_price_spread_tobacco
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_plantation
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_tobacco >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_tobacco }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_tobacco }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_tobacco = 1
spec_review_has_tobacco = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_tobacco = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_plantation } value = var:spec_review_plantation } }
spec_review_reduction_tobacco = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_tobacco = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_tobacco divide = spec_price_spread_tobacco }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_tobacco = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_tobacco
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_tobacco >= var:spec_slow_tobacco }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_tobacco = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_tobacco >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_tobacco
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_tobacco
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_tobacco = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tobacco spec_mv_tobacco > 0 var:spec_term_tobacco > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tobacco spec_mv_tobacco > 0 var:spec_term_tobacco < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_tobacco = { value = spec_dev_tobacco multiply = spec_term_side_tobacco divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_tobacco = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_tobacco } value = var:spec_term_tobacco multiply = spec_term_side_tobacco divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_tobacco = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_tobacco add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_tobacco = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_tobacco divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_tobacco = { value = spec_term_supply_tobacco divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_tobacco = { value = spec_term_side_tobacco multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_tobacco multiply = spec_term_fade_tobacco multiply = spec_term_conf_tobacco multiply = spec_term_share_tobacco }

spec_term_tp_tobacco = { value = spec_term_effect_tobacco multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_tobacco = { value = spec_term_effect_tobacco add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_tobacco = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_tobacco
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_tobacco has_variable = spec_restrain_tobacco }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_tobacco
	}
}

spec_push_next_tobacco = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_encourage_tobacco }
		value = spec_push_full_encourage
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_tobacco }
		value = spec_push_full_restrain
	}
	subtract = spec_push_sv_tobacco
	multiply = 0.35
	add = spec_push_sv_tobacco
}

spec_hype_next_tobacco = {
	value = spec_hype_raw_tobacco
	multiply = spec_sentiment_factor_tobacco
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_glut_tobacco }
		value = 0.4
	}
	multiply = spec_push_next_tobacco
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_tobacco }
		min = 1
	}
}

spec_peak_next_tobacco = {
	value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
	multiply = 0.97
	add = 0.03
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_restrain_tobacco }
		value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
		multiply = 0.9
		add = 0.1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_hype_tobacco > var:spec_peak_tobacco }
		value = var:spec_hype_tobacco
	}
}

spec_restrain_cost_tobacco = {
	value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
	subtract = spec_peak_next_tobacco
	min = 0
	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_tobacco
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_tobacco
}

spec_sev_next_tobacco = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_tobacco = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_tobacco
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_tobacco
		multiply = spec_over_mult_tobacco
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_tobacco
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_tobacco
		multiply = spec_over_mult_tobacco
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_tobacco >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_tobacco
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_tobacco
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_tobacco
		multiply = spec_over_mult_tobacco
	}
}

spec_risk_next_tobacco = {
	value = spec_risk_raw_tobacco
	multiply = spec_crash_scale
	max = 35
}

spec_price_spread_wood = {
    value = var:spec_fast_wood
    subtract = var:spec_slow_wood
}
spec_sector_slice_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_sector_share_materials } value = var:spec_sector_share_materials } }
spec_supported_spread_wood = {
    value = spec_price_spread_wood
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_cat_trend_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_cat_trend_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_wood >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_wood }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_wood }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_spread_wood = {
    value = spec_price_spread_wood
    if = {
        limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials }
        multiply = 0.5
        add = {
            value = var:spec_review_materials
            if = { limit = { spec_price_spread_wood >= 0 } min = 0 max = spec_price_spread_wood }
            else = { max = 0 min = spec_price_spread_wood }
            multiply = 0.5
        }
    }
}
spec_review_sector_wood = 3
spec_review_has_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials } value = 1 } }
spec_review_mean_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_review_materials } value = var:spec_review_materials } }
spec_review_reduction_wood = {
    value = 0
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { spec_price_spread_wood = 0 } }
        value = 1
        subtract = { value = spec_review_spread_wood divide = spec_price_spread_wood }
        min = 0
        max = 0.5
    }
}

spec_trend_wood = {
	value = spec_supported_spread_wood
	multiply = 60
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_fast_wood >= var:spec_slow_wood }
		multiply = var:spec_hm
	}
	else = {
		multiply = var:spec_hmd
	}
}

spec_hype_raw_wood = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = { spec_trend_wood >= 0 }
		add = spec_trend_wood
	}
	else = {
		divide = {
			value = 1
			subtract = spec_trend_wood
		}
	}
	min = 0.05
	max = 20
}

spec_term_side_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_wood spec_mv_wood > 0 var:spec_term_wood > 0 } value = 1 } else_if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_wood spec_mv_wood > 0 var:spec_term_wood < 0 } value = -1 } }

spec_term_gap_wood = { value = spec_dev_wood multiply = spec_term_side_wood divide = 0.2 min = 0 }

spec_term_fade_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_term_wood } value = var:spec_term_wood multiply = spec_term_side_wood divide = 6 max = 1 } }

spec_term_conf_wood = { value = 1 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_conf } value = var:spec_conf subtract = 50 divide = 50 multiply = 0.25 multiply = spec_term_side_wood add = 1 } }

spec_term_supply_wood = { value = 0 if = { limit = { var:spec_mv_total > 0 } value = spec_mv_wood divide = var:spec_mv_total } }

spec_term_share_wood = { value = spec_term_supply_wood divide = 0.02 subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 }

spec_term_effect_wood = { value = spec_term_side_wood multiply = 0.1 multiply = spec_term_gap_wood multiply = spec_term_fade_wood multiply = spec_term_conf_wood multiply = spec_term_share_wood }

spec_term_tp_wood = { value = spec_term_effect_wood multiply = 0.5 }

spec_sentiment_factor_wood = { value = spec_term_effect_wood add = 1 min = 0 }

spec_push_sv_wood = {
	value = 1
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_push_wood
			OR = { has_variable = spec_encourage_wood has_variable = spec_restrain_wood }
		}
		value = var:spec_push_wood
	}
}

	add = spec_push_sv_wood

	subtract = spec_peak_next_wood

	multiply = 1.25
	multiply = spec_weight_wood
	multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood

	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 6 }
		value = 3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 3 }
		value = 2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 2 }
		value = 1
	}
}

spec_risk_raw_wood = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 6 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 7
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 3 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 2.5
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:spec_peak_wood >= 2 }
		value = var:spec_peak_wood
		subtract = 1
		multiply = 1.25
		multiply = spec_weight_wood
		multiply = spec_exp_mult_wood
		multiply = spec_over_mult_wood
	}
}
